# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-09 through 2026-10-15  
**Updated:** 2026-10-09T10:41:37+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-02-06

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-09 — Back to the Future outdoor movie night + Good Time Country Buffet

**Back to the Future outdoor movie night, then dinner.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Hudson+Yards%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=166+First+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.hudsonyardsnewyork.com/movie-nights) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/) · [Reservations](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### 2026-10-11 — Cozy and Crafted Bazaar + Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi

**Cozy and Crafted Bazaar, then a meal.** Afro-Caribbean American at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=100+W+77th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10024&destination=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://grandbazaarnyc.org/events/cozy-and-crafted-bazaar/) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-10-10 — Seaport Music Festival + Dean's

**Seaport Music Festival, then dinner.** British pub at Dean's. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It runs to 10 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Fulton+St+%26+Water+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10038&destination=20+King+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.instagram.com/seaportmusicfestival/)

### 2026-10-11 — Pioneer Works Second Sundays + Gage & Tollner

**Pioneer Works Second Sundays, then a meal.** American steakhouse at Gage & Tollner. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=372+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pioneerworks.org/programs/second-sundays-october-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/)

### 2026-10-11 — Roy Lichtenstein: Like New + Good Time Country Buffet

**Roy Lichtenstein: Like New, then a meal.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=99+Gansevoort+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&destination=166+First+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/lichtenstein) · [Event booking](https://whitney.org/admission) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/) · [Reservations](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### 2026-10-15 — Seeing the Cinema Thursday free-hours visit + Adda Indian Canteen

**Seeing the Cinema Thursday free-hours visit, then a meal.** Indian at Adda Indian Canteen. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=36-01+35th+Ave%2C+Astoria%2C+NY+11106&destination=31-31+Thomson+Ave%2C+Long+Island+City%2C+NY+11101&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://movingimage.org/event/seeing-the-cinema/) · [Event booking](https://movingimage.org/visit/) · [Restaurant](https://www.addanyc.com/)

### 2026-10-09 — Claud + Vocabaret: Magic

**Dinner, then Vocabaret: Magic.** New American wine bar at Claud. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. Vocabaret: Magic starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=90+E+10th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=21+A+Clinton+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://caveat.nyc/events/vocabaret-2026-10-09) · [Restaurant](https://www.claudnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-10 — Le Rock + Carnegie Hill Concerts: Jaleel Shaw

**Dinner, then Carnegie Hill Concerts: Jaleel Shaw.** French at Le Rock. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Carnegie Hill Concerts: Jaleel Shaw starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=45+Rockefeller+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10111&destination=111+E+87th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10128&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.carnegiehillconcerts.org/concerts/2026/10-10-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.lerocknyc.com/)

### 2026-10-09

#### [New York African Restaurant Week Festival](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/2026-new-york-african-restaurant-week-festival-oct-9-11-tickets-1986132308199)

12:00 PM · food · Free entry; food for purchase · Restoration Plaza · Bed-Stuy · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Free marketplace festival with 40-plus food, fashion, and small-business vendors representing Senegal, Nigeria, Ghana, Haiti, Sierra Leone, Ivory Coast, Jamaica, Trinidad, and more.
**Timing:** Three-day festival October 9-11; daily hours not fully exposed by fetch.
**Practical note:** Mostly fair Friday and Saturday; Sunday has a high rain chance, so check covered areas before committing.
**Why now:** Runs October 9-11 during African Restaurant Week, with a Sunday jollof tasting contest.
**On-sale watch:** Food vouchers are while supplies last.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 49.0  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.
[Official](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.

**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.

## Where to eat

I verified the risky dining claims against the captured public evidence and kept sourced, current restaurant-specific items while pruning duplicate or unsupported deal cards.

Dining research: 2026-10-09T10:33:50+00:00

No candidate contained a Michelin star or Michelin Guide selection claim in the supplied fields, and I added no award/recognition claims. Public evidence reviewed here did not require a Michelin source check.

### Good Time Country Buffet opened as an East Village Southern buffet

New openings — Good Time Country Buffet

Eater reported the team behind Kisa and C as in Charlie opened an all-you-can-eat country buffet with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rib tips, catfish, salad bar, and rotating specials.

Price: Adults $26.99; 65+ $23.99; kids 4-9 $13.99; desserts and boozy drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus a limited number of reservations through Resy reported; tax, tip, service charges, and exact current reservation inventory not stated in fetched text.  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Good Time Country Buffet all-you-can-eat dinner pricing

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

A rare lower-priced all-you-can-eat sit-down dinner option in Manhattan, useful for groups who want predictable pricing and comfort-food variety.

Price: Adults $26.99; 65+ $23.99; kids 4-9 $13.99; sweet tea/soda for a small extra fee per Resy; desserts and alcohol extra per Eater  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Dinner only at opening; Resy says lunch is planned later but not available at the checked source. Tax/tip/service charges unknown.  
From: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### Good Time Country Buffet Southern buffet

More restaurants — Good Time Country Buffet

Menu focus is straightforward Southern buffet food rather than Korean riffs: fried chicken, collard greens, mac and cheese, corn, mashed potatoes, salad bar, banana pudding, and rotating specials.

Price: Included in buffet price except drinks/desserts where stated as extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday dinner service, 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Rotating specials may change; takeout was described by Resy as planned for later, not available at launch. This is the restaurant's regular buffet format, not a separate special menu. Sources: https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/ and https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa  
From: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### La Grande Boucherie $45 lunch prix fixe

Deals & happy hours — La Grande Boucherie

Official menu lists a weekday two-course-style lunch prix fixe with French onion soup/Caesar/pate starters and mains including mushroom ravioli or chicken paillard, with steak supplement.

Price: Lunch Prix Fixe $45; Bavette Persillade +$10  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 11:00 AM-4:00 PM  
Conditions: Available at La Grande Boucherie NYC per menu creator/location. Tax, tip, beverages, substitutions, and service charges not stated in fetched text.  
[Source](https://www.boucherieus.com/la-grande-boucherie-menus/)

### La Grande Boucherie late-lunch menu window

Special menus — La Grande Boucherie

A narrow daily late-lunch menu can be useful between standard lunch and dinner, with oysters, shrimp cocktail, French onion soup, burger, steak frites, and wines by the glass.

Price: Examples: oysters $30 half dozen; shrimp cocktail $35/4 pc; French onion soup $23; Boucherie burger $38; prime steak frites $58; white wine $19; red wine $22  
Schedule: Monday-Sunday 3:45 PM-4:45 PM  
Conditions: This is a special time-window menu, not a discount claim. Tax, tip, service charges, and reservation requirements unknown.  
[Source](https://www.boucherieus.com/la-grande-boucherie-menus/)

### Queens Night Market final-season Saturdays

Pop-ups — Queens Night Market

Bounded outdoor food market with 100+ independent vendors and small-scale cultural performances; next market after research date is 2026-10-10, with 2026 season continuing through 2026-10-31.

Price: Admission free for the current fall markets per official FAQ; vendor food prices unknown; adjacent parking operator charges $15 per vehicle, subject to change.  
Schedule: Saturdays, 4:00 PM–midnight; 2026 fall run September 19–October 31  
Conditions: Recurring market with a bounded fall run and next event date; not a fixed vendor menu. Most vendors primarily accept cash, some accept cards/Zelle/Venmo/Apple Pay; rain or shine except unsafe weather. Sources: https://queensnightmarket.com/ and https://queensnightmarket.com/faq  
Event date: 2026-10-10  
From: 2026-09-19  
Through: 2026-10-31  
[Source](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

Price: Free entry; food/drink prices unknown

Event date: 2026-10-10  
From: 2026-10-10

Conditions: Official menu verifies the $20 double cheeseburger is limited: dinner says best chance before 6 PM; weekend brunch says likely gone by 1 PM. Classified as a restaurant card with a limited regular menu item, not a discount. Source: https://www.rolosnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.rolosnyc.com/menus/)
